- **learn-repo**: Learn an unfamiliar repository or codebase (or any technical topic) by being taught and quizzed on it, read-only. Use this skill when the user wants to LEARN or understand a repo, codebase, framework, or concept — not change it. Opens with a full explanation of the whole repo (architecture, entry points, how the pieces fit), then runs a four-stage loop: Learn (first principles, analogies) → Check understanding (one question at a time, adaptive difficulty) → Practical application (real examples) → Feynman (learner explains back, gaps identified).
